4a1(rgyud): @#/ O /rig pa bye ba phrag du mas yongasu bskor nas/'khor de nyid du 'dus te/bcoM

4b2(rgyud): par gyur te/rgyal po chen po bzhi yang g.yog 'khor bye ba phrag du mas kun nas bskor te/'khor de nyid du 'dus nas/bcoM ldan 'das kyi zhab+sa la phyag 'tshal 
4b3(rgyud): te/phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' lag na rdo rjes bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar te/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa

5a2(rgyud): lag na rdo rje khyod ni shinadu brtsam pa med pa shin du gtum pa yin na/ji ltar sems can rnams kyi don gyi phyir sngags 'chad/lag na 
5a3(rgyud): rdo rje khyod ni dam tshig shes pa yin la/sems can gang dag dam tshig mi shes par brjod pa rnams ni rdo rjes mthar byed yin na/khyod 
5a4(rgyud): ji ltar gsang sngags 'chad par gnang bar gsol/bcom ldan 'das de ni yin mod kyi/bcom ldan 'das gang dag rig pa'i tshogs 
5a5(rgyud): 'di 'don pa dang /'chang ba dang /mchod pa bgyid pa de dag ni/bdag gi dam tshig 'tshal ba lags/bdag gi dkyil 'khor du zhugs 
5b1(rgyud): pa lags pas/bcom ldan 'das kyi gnang bar mdzad du gsol/gnod sbyin gyi bdag po lag na rdo rjes 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 
5b2(rgyud): bdag gi yi ge bzhi pa zhes bya ba 'di ni rig pa'i mchog thams cad kyi snying por gyur pa/las mang po rab du bsgrub pa/stobs kyis pha rol gnon pa 
5b3(rgyud): dpag tu ma mchis pa/bstan pa thogs pa ma mchis pa ste/bcom ldan 'das rig pa'i rgyal po bshad par bgyi'o//kye kye lha yul dang /sa dang chu na 
5b4(rgyud): gnas pa'i 'byung po'i tshogs rnams/khyod yid brtse gcig tu gyis la nyon cig//rdo rjes mgo bo bkas par gyur ta re/de nas lag na rdo rje rang gi 
5b5(rgyud): rig pa'i tshogs la bltas nas gsang sngags smras pa/na mo rad na tra ya y+ya/s+ya na ma Sh+tsa Da badzra/pa na s+ya/mahA yag Sha se na pa ta s+ya/nA ma shAkya mu 
5b6(rgyud): ne saM bud d+ha ya/bi rli ka rli ma rli ha rli/bid d+yu+od ta ma ya rtsa rma ni/du rmi Ni la swAhA//de nas thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de 'dar yed nas/thal mo 
6a1(rgyud): @#//sbyar te/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/bcom ldan 'das bdag cag la bskyab du gsol/bde bar gshegs pa 
6a2(rgyud): bdag cag la bskyab du gso//de nas gnod sbyin gyi bdag po lag na rdo rjes thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor la/grogs po dag ma 'jigs shig/ma 'jigs shig/
6a3(rgyud): rig pa'i rgyal po dran par gyis shig ces dbugs phyung ngo //de nas thams cad dang ldan pa'i'akhor de ngo mtshar du gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das la 'diskad ces gsol 
6a4(rgyud): to//bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po//lag na rdo rje 'di lta bu'i gsang sngags kyi tshig 'chad pa ni ngo mtshar to//bcom ldan 
6a5(rgyud): 'das bdag cag ni rig pa'i rgyal po gang na gnas pa der mchod par mtshal lo//su klog pa dang /mchod par byed pa dang /bsgrub pa'i rig sngags 'chang ba de'i brtson 
6a6(rgyud): 'grus bskyed par bgyi'o//bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu dag de de bzhin te/de de bzhin no//de nas rig pa'i rgyal po mchog gi las ni 'di lags te/srad bu 'am/
6b1(rgyud): thal ba 'am/chu la lan gcig brjod pas bsrung bar 'gyur ro//rig sngags gcod na/mtshon rnon po la lan gnyis brjod de rtswa ku shas gcad do//yang na'ang tra'o de ltar rig 
6b2(rgyud): sngags gcod par 'gyur ro//lan gsum brjod na rig sngags tshangs pa'i 'gyur te/mar gyi ngo zhag brgyad la 'phags pa lag na rdo rje'i mdun du mchod pa bgyis la rig sngags 
6b3(rgyud): gang yin ma tshang ba 'am/dran pa nyams pa de lan brgya rtsa brgyad brjod do//rig pa mchog gi snying po dang lhan cig du lan gsum brjod na/rmi lam du ston to//ci ste las bcad 
6b4(rgyud): par gyur na/rtsa ku shas byab par byas ste/lag na rdo rje'i mdun du lan bzhi brjod na dge ba dang /mi dge ba ston par 'gyur ro//ci 'dod pa bzhin du sbyor ba bgyis na/las rnams 
6b5(rgyud): su 'gyur ro//gdon tshar gcad na thal ba 'am/chu 'am/dur khrod gyi sol bas bsnun no//yang na gzugs bgyis te/lcug mas gzhu na gtong bar 'gyur ro//yang na sa la 
6b6(rgyud): gzugs bgyis la dur khrod kyi sol bas/lag mthil la rdo rje bris la/lag pas bkab na gtong bar 'gyur ro//skud pa la mdud pa bor ba rnams kyis rig sngags bcing ngo //
6b7(rgyud): mkha'agro ma rnams gcod par bgyi'o//yungs kar la lan bdun sngags te/bsnun na mkha'agro ma rnams ma rungs par 'gyur ro//dbab par bgyis na rna bar bzlas brjod bya'o//
7a1(rgyud): rig mchog

7b1(rgyud): bshig par 'gyur te/las mi bgyid do//rig pa dgug par 'tshal bas mar gyi ngo zhag brgyad la nyin zhag gcig snyung bar bgyis te/lag na rdo rje'i mdun du mchod

7b3(rgyud): bgyi ste/bzlas brjod byas pas chog ste/che ge mo'i rig pa la gnod par mdzod cig ces bgyis na/rig pa de las mi bgyid do//rig pa'i tshar gcad par 
7b4(rgyud): bgyid na/thal ba la gzugs bgyis te/rig pa mchog gi snying pa dang lhan cig brjod la/rkang pa g.yon pas kyang phyis na rig pa smad pa dang /nyams par

7b7(rgyud): go/rig sngags 'chang gi de'i tshe ci tsam par/gsang sngags rnams mi 'grub po//slar gso bar bgyi bar bgyi na/ma la ti'i me tog mar la bsnyugs 
8a1(rgyud): rig mchog @#/ O /pa'i sbyin bsreg bgyi ba/lan nyi shu rtsa gcig lag na rdo rje'i mdun du sbyin bsreg bgyis na 'grub po//las 'di dag ni ma bsgrubs kyang

11b5(rgyud): stags la ni yid kyis dkyil 'khor bgyis na bcings par 'gyur ro//mtshams gcad na yungs kar gyis so//zhing bsrung na phur bus dag gis so//sman bsrung na yid kyis rig pa mchog 
11b6(rgyud): sbyar bar bgyi'o//gzhan yang bsrubs pa ni ma gon pa'i ras la spyin dang ma 'dres pa'i tshon rtsi rnams kyis/rig pa'i rgyal po mchog 'di 'bri bar bgyis te/mdog sngo bsangs sham thabs 
11b7(rgyud): ser po//lag pa g.yon pa na zhags pa g.yas pa dam pa sbyin par bri'o//'og tu sgrub pa po pog phor thogs pa bri'o//zhag bdun du zas su 'o skyo btung ngo //tha mar zhag gsum du bsnyung ba 
11b8(rgyud): bgyis te/mar gyi ngo'i zhag bcu bzhi la ci nus kyis mchod pa bgyis la/skra tshogs la zhags las bgyis te/lag pa g.yas par thogs la/ci srid du rung gi gzugs kyi mchi ba de srid du 
12a1(rgyud): @#/ O /bzlas brjod bgyi'o//rdo rje bshad mas smras bzhug ste/de bu ci bya zhes smra bar 'gyur ro//de tangs pa 'am khyab 'jug gam dbang phyug chen po 'am/che ge mo zhes bya ba zhags 
12a2(rgyud): pas bzung ste/khrid de spyon zhes brjod par bya'o//yang rgyal po yis/gnod sbyin srin po 'am/sha za 'am/gang dgug par 'ded pa de dgug ste/de dag thams cad phyag brnyan du 'gyur ro//gang I
12a3(rgyud): bsam pa de 'tshal te 'chi'o//yang mgon po'i dus la rim mkhan gso sbyong bzung bas/spyin dang ma 'dres pa'i tshon rtsi rnams kyis rig pa'i rgyal po mchog bri bar bya ste/mdog sde bsangs 
12a4(rgyud): rgyan thams cad kyi brgyan I sta dge yogs dar dkar/spad g.yegs ras/lag pa bzhi pa lag pa g.yon pa gcig na rdo rje/gcig na lcags kyu/g.yas pa cig dam pa sbyin pa/cig be con 
12a5(rgyud): thogs pa bri bar bya'o//'og tu sgrub pa po/phog por thegs pa bri'o//g.yas su rdo rje khu tshur/mdog ser lta bu/rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa lag pa gnyis na rdo rje thogs pa bri'o//
12a6(rgyud): g.yon du blo gros rdo rje bri bar bgyis te/de yang mdog gser lta bu/rgyan thams cadakyis brgyan pa'o//de nas rdo rje bsgrub pa ni/zla ba gcig bslongs me pa'i zas zos la/tha mar lus 
12a7(rgyud): sbyang bar bgyis ste/tshe 'dul gyi zla ba ca la zhag gsum bsnyung bar bgyi ste/lcags sna gsum la bgyis pa'i rdo rje/ri mo'i sku gzugs kyi mdun du/shing a shod ta'i lo ma bdun gyi steng 
12b1(rgyud): du bzhag la/bsgrub ste ci srid kyis 'bar ba de srid du bzlas brjod bgyi'o//'bar bas blang bar bgyi ste/blangs ma thag du lag na rdo rje 'dra bar 'gyur/nyi ma shar ba'i kha dog 'dra bar
12b2(rgyud): 'gyur ro//rig sngags 'chang gi 'khor los bsgyur bar 'gyur ro//de nas lcags kyu bsgrub na sngar spyad par bgyi ste/rtsa ku sha rtsa med pa las lcags kyu bgyis la mar gyi ngo zhag
12b3(rgyud): bcu bzhi pa zhag gsum bsnyung bar bgyi ste/ri mo'i sku gzugs kyi mdun du mchod pa/rgya cher bgyis la me tog gi steng du bzhag ste/de'i steng du lag pa bzhag la ji srid gyi
12b4(rgyud): bar de srid du bzlas brjod bgya'o//'bar na blang bar bgyis te/blangs ma thag du sa ser ga mthong zhing /tshangs pa'i gnas su 'chi bar 'gyur ro//lag na lcags kyu thogs nas sum
12b5(rgyud): bcu rtsa gsum pas kyang zil kyis gnon par mi nus so//be con bsgrub pa bshad par bgyid de/lcags na gsum las be con sor bcu drug pa/dbyug to 'dra ba zur drug par bgyi'o//sngar
12b6(rgyud): spyad par bgyis la/mar gyi ngo zhag bcu bzhi la zhag gsum bsnyung ba bgyis te/ri mo'i sku mdun du mchod pa rgya chen po bgyis la/ras dkar po'i steng du gzhag ste/ji srid 'bar ba de
12b7(rgyud): srid du bzlas brjod bgyi'o//de nas blangs ma thag du lag na rdo rje 'dra bar 'gyur te/lag na rdo rje dang stobs mnyam zhing lag na rdo rje'i gnas su mchi bar 'gyur ro//dngos grub rnam pa gsum
13a1(rgyud): rig mchog
13a2(rgyud): rig mchog

13a4(rgyud): rig pa'i rgyal po 'don pa dang /mchod pa des de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la mchod pa lags so//mnyes par bgyis pa lags so//thod pa blangs ste khrag gis bkang la/
13a5(rgyud): mar gyi ngo zhag brgyad la nyin gcig bsnyung ba bgyis te/khang stong ngam kun dga' ra bar ci srid gyis bud med 'byung ba de srid du bzlas pa bgyi'o//de la che ge mo zhig la gnod pa gyis shig ces
13a6(rgyud): brjod na gnod pa bgyid do//gnod par bgyid du 'jug go/rengs par bgyid do//rig pa'i rgyal po 'di dgug pa 'dis bkug nas phyis rgyal ba'i blo gros dgug par bgyi'o//na mo rad+na
13a7(rgyud): tra pA s+ya/na many+tsa da badzra pA ni s+ya/mahA yak+sha se na pra ti s+ya/tu ru tu ru/tsu ru tsu ru/ki ri ki ri/rA+au trA pra tsa na d+ha ya/tsan d+ha ya/a gad tsha a gad tsha/he b+ha ga wa/a gad tsha a 
13a8(rgyud): gad tsha/ma ma a the ya b+ha ri pu ra ye a pa krid b+hid d+h+ya ra dzod ta a ham/rbaM a kar Sha ya/ya mi na ma sarba ar+tha ma nor Sha pa ri pU ri/ka b+ha ba mA ma ar+tha si d+hin tsa d+ha da mA tsi ra na
13a9(rgyud): ku ru swAhA//ci ste tshes brgyad la bsgrub par 'tshal na/tshes bdun gyi tshe/phyi 'phred gu gul gyi bdug pas gdugs la/gsang sngags lan brgyad rgya brjod do//bsgrub pa na rgyal ba'i
13a10(rgyud): blo gros gyis dgug go/bsgrub pa thams cad dang /las thams cad du yang dgug pa 'dis dgug go/na mo rad na tra ya s+ya/na maSh+tsa na d+ha badzra pa na s+ya/mahA yak+Sha se na pa ta s+ya/dza ya 
13b1(rgyud): ma ti/dzi na ma ti/dzi na d+ha ri ni/a ba sha ya/dash+Ta ma da sh+Ta ma b+ha/da ba na ga yak+Sha gan d+ha rbe/kU nu tsa ra ma nu Sha ma/la hU~M la hU~M/grih+na grih+na/b+hu ma ma pa ta ya shi g+han badzra pA ni sa~M ad nya 
13b2(rgyud): pa ya ti swAhA//rgyal ba'i blo gros 'dis 'gug par bya'o//de nas rdo rje lcags sgrog gis bcing bar bgyi'o//na mo rad na tra ya yA/na ma sh+tsan da badzra pA na ye/mahA yak+Sha sa na pa ta ye/
13b3(rgyud): ku ru ku ru/tsu Da tsu Da/ma la ma la/sa ra sa ra/ti sh+tha ti sh+tha/ma ta ba la/sa ma ya ma nu smar sid d+ha sh+tsan d+ha badzra pA ni/ad nya pa ya ti swAhA//rdo rje lcags sgrog 'dis gu gul gyi ri lu gsuM
13b4(rgyud): yungs kar gyi 'bru mar du snyugs la/sbyin bsreg bgyis na bcings par 'gyur/rig pa bsgrub pa dang /pho nya mo yang rung ste/de 'di nyid kyis bcings par bgyi'o//rdo rje ser mos
13b5(rgyud): ni bcing pa las grol bar bgyi'o//na mo rad ni tra ya ya/na ma tsan d+ha badzra pA na ye/mahA ya k+Sha se nu pa te ye/hu lu hu lu/gad tsha gad tsha/mahA pA la swAhA ba nam/bu ra na ra u
13b6(rgyud): ga ma na ya/ku ru ku ru/gad tsha gad tsha swAhA//chab khyor ba gsum gtor ro//me tog kyang 'bul lo//de nas shugs ldan gyis slar gshegs par gsol lo//na mo badzra ba na
13b7(rgyud): ye/pa Ta pa Ta/gad tsha gad tsha/be ga ba ti/mahA ya sha d+hi b+ha ti/tsi ri li/hi ri me/a bag nang ku ru swAhA//shugs ldan gyis slar gsheg pa gsol ba'i sngags te/
13b8(rgyud): a ka ru'i bdug pas lan bdun bzlas brjod byas so//na mo badzra pA ni s+ya/na ma ste/b+ha ga ba ti/badzra mu li/go'u ri bag tre/ke ra te ke ra Te/ki ni Ti ki ni Ti/nye reg bya ba'i
14a1(rgyud): @#/ O /gsang sngags gsungs so//na mo badzra pA ni s+ya/dza la badzra na bi hu ru hu ru/la hU la hU/pra ti gri n+ha tam/a nam gan d+ham/gi li gi la ya swAhA//spos gyi 
14a2(rgyud): sngags so//na mo badzra pA nI s+ya/da ha da ha/pa tsa pa tsa/b+han d+ha b+han d+ha/shi li swAhA//me tog gi gsang sngags so//na mo badzra bA ni s+ya/a ti ni tsi li ni/kha la gi mi ni/d+ha ra 
14a3(rgyud): tsi li ni/tsi li ni swAhA//bdug pa'i sngags so//na mo badzra pI na s+ya/na mo ste b+ha ga ba ti she ta mu kha'i swAhA go ru ri/tsan tsu la le tsu tsu le/tsi tsi rak+Sha rak+Sha swAhA//srad bus bsrung ba 
14a4(rgyud): bgyi'o//na mo badzra pA ni s+ya/ka Ta ka Ta/ma Ta ma Ta/ra ra ra ra badzra bing ga la/d+ha ra d+ha ra/ni ki ri ni/ki ri ni ki ri swAhA//khrus bgyi ba'i gsang sngags so//na mo badzra pA ni s+ya/
14a5(rgyud): na mo ste ba ga pa ti/da shi pa ti pa dang pra pa ti/a gad tsha/shi g+han/ki ri ki ri/b+han d+ha b+han d+ha swA hA//srad bu la lan bdun bzlas brjod bgyis te khu ba bcing ngo //na mo badzra pA 
14a6(rgyud): ni s+ya/u d+ha ma ra mak+Sha/a da rak+Sha/tsa dur ti sham ra rak+Sha/ma tha ma tha/tra sa ya tra sa ya/staM b+ha ya staM b+ha ya/ud tri ta/badzra ad nya pa ya ti swAhA//yungs kar la sngag+sa 
14a7(rgyud): nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas brjod bgyis te/phyogs bcing ngo //na mo badzra pA ni s+ya/tad+ya thA badzra badzra na/badzra sing ha sa ni/badzra bA ni bri ye/badzra pA ni ba ra te/ba kri ta dang sh+Ta ka ra la/ba da 
14b1(rgyud): ne laM b+ho d+ha ri/bi kri ta kro ra ru ba dar sha ne/ma dza ra bag tre man dza ma la bri ye/e hi e hi/b+ha ga ba ti/di bi gra ru ba/dza li ta kra/krI shi i dam nu sha sha ri ri ma nu bri bi sha//ka Ta 
14b2(rgyud): ka Ta/bU ru bU ru/d+hu na d+hu na/pa Ta ba Ta/balgu balgu/ha sa ha sa/A bi sha A bi sha/s+phaT s+phaT/kan tsi ra ya sa/b+ha ga ba ti/ba ru ru be/aT bring ka/shi ka la he ru/
14b3(rgyud): ka ra bi te/ad Dad Da da se/i ma s+ya du sh+ta kri ha s+ya/hri da ya~M/b+hin d+ha bin d+ha/tsa la tsa la/gu mu gu mu/ku ru ku ru/a bi sha/a bi sha/badzra pa ni ad nya pa ti swAhA//
14b4(rgyud): las thams cad du ku shi sbams la sngags gyi btab ste bdug par bgyi'o//na mo badzra pA ni s+ya/la la ha sa ha sa/badzri ni ku ru ku ru/ka~M pa ka~M pa/tsa la tsa la dza wa dza wa swAhA//gshegs 
14b5(rgyud): nas gsung du bzhugs pa'o//na mo badzra pA ni se ya d+hu d+hu/ma la sa la ma la sa/du du me/a ma le/badzra pA ni ma kA d+ha ba ti/ma tsa me ka sh+tsir big nad ku ru swAhA//yungs kar ram/
14b6(rgyud): thal bas bgeg+sa rab tu zhi bar bgyi'o//ci ste rig sngags 'chang grub ba rnams kyi rig sngags thams cad gcad par 'gyur ro//ma gon pa'i ras la spyin dang ma 'dres pa'i tshon rtsi rnam+sa 
14b7(rgyud): kyis rig pa'i mchog bri bar bgyis te/mdog sngo bsangs rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa'o//sbyin bsreg bgyi ba bza' zhing bri bar bgyi'o//mar gyi ngo zhag bcu bzhi la/zhag gcig 
15a1(rgyud): @#/ O /bsnyung bar bgyis te/ri mo'i sku gzugs kyi mdun du ci nus su mchod pa bgyis la byang chub kyi shing gi zhugs phul te/ma la ta'i me tog mar la snyugs p+sa 
15a2(rgyud): brgya stong sbyin bsreg bgyis na/ri mo'i sku gzugs 'gul lo//me tog 'tshal na/mar gyi tshes bcwo lnga la/lag na rdo rje'i mdun du/ba gu la'i me tog dag las lan brgya 
15a3(rgyud): stong sbyin bsreg bgyis na/rig sngags 'chang thams cad gyi rig sngags thams cad gcod par gi phreng ba yang 'gul lo//de nas mtshan mo'i lha mo me tog ces bgyi ba grub 
15a4(rgyud): par 'gyur te/lha mo me tog gi las rnams su 'gyur ro//rig sngags 'chang gi rig pa bsgrub pa la bgegs su mi 'gyur ro//log 'dren gyis mi tshugs so//dngos grub 'dis 
15a5(rgyud): mtshan mo'i lha mo bzhi la dngos grub tu 'gyur ro//srod ni lha mo me tog gis so//'dzing ni lha mo phreng ba'o//nam gung ni lha mo ku mu ta'o//tho rangs ni lha mo mdzes ma'o//
15a6(rgyud): rig sngags 'chang gi rig par bsgrub na/srod la log 'dren gnas su 'ongs ste/de nas mtshan mo me tog gis 'dre mthong nas/bcom ldan 'das lag na rdo rje yid la bgyi'o//lag 
15a7(rgyud): na rdo rje yid la bgyis pas/log 'dren rnams skrag cing nyams ste/nyams thag nas 'bros par 'gyur ro//lag na rdo rje'i rig sngags 'chang de'i mdun du 'dug cing dngos grub 
15a8(rgyud): kyang myur du stsol bar bgyid do//'jing la gal te bgegs byung na mtshan mo sa'i lha mo phreng ba des lha chen po la/bcom ldan 'das lag nas rdo rje khyod la 'khod do zhes sgrogs so//
15b1(rgyud): de des thos nas snying 'dar zhing khu ba myur bar mdun du 'dug ste/de nas rig sngags 'chang de la bgegs su mi 'gyur ro//dngos grub kyang myur du 'grub po//nam gung la gal te 
15b2(rgyud): bgegs byung na/mtshan mo'i lha mo ku mu ta de yang /rgyal po chen po bzhi la la kye kye/btsun kon dag lag na rdo rje khyod la mchod do zhes sgrogs so//de dag gi tshig de thos nas snying 
15b3(rgyud): 'dar zhing myur bar 'dug ste/des na de la bgegasu mi 'gyur ro//tho rangs la gal te bgegs byung na mtshan mo'i lha mo mdzes ma yang log 'dren rnams la/kye kye log 'dren 
15b4(rgyud): snang ba rnams drag po lag na rdo rjes bka' stsal ba/zhes sgo bar bgyi'o//de dag gis de'i tshig de thos nas phyogs bcur bros so//de nas bgegs su mi 'gyur ro//bde ba'i las 
15b5(rgyud): rnams grub par byed/mar gyi ngo zhag bdun nam bcu bzhi la bud med bu byung ba'i khyim gyi lte ba sor gnyis tsam gcad la gser gyi khyim bag gis dkris te/mar gyi ngo zhag brgyad 
15b6(rgyud): la nyin gnyis bsnyung bar bgyis te/lag na rdo rje'i mdun du lan brgya stong bzlas brjod bgyis nas mi snang bar 'gyur ro//tshe yang nyis 'gyur du 'gyur ro//zhag bdun du phye thug btung ngo //
15b7(rgyud): phyis mar gyi ngo zhag bcu bzhi nas/zhag gsum bsnyung ba bgyis te/bzhi ba'i mtshan yod par song la/mtshan gyi steng gi lho phyogs logs su lag pa g.yas pa ba zhag ste/
15b8(rgyud): skra las bgyis pa'i skud pas bcing ngo //bcings nas ji srid kyis gcad do//zhes bya ba'i sgra byung ba de srid du bzlas brjod bgyi'o//sgra lan gsum du byung na dngos grub du 'gyur ro//
16a1(rgyud): @#/ O /khu tshur bcangs te bsgrub par bgyi'o//khu tshur bcangs na mi snang bar 'gyur ro//skra lcang lor 'khyil bar 'gyur ro//lo bcu drug lon pa tsam du 'gyur ro//
16a2(rgyud): lo bdun brgyar 'tsho bar 'gyur ro//dbang phyug chen po'i tshogs kyang phyag brnyan du 'gyur ro//ci ste dgra bsad par 'tshal na mar gyi ngo zhag bcu bzhi la nyin zhag gcig 
16a3(rgyud): bsnyung bar bgyis te/spra tshil la gzugs bgyis te/seng ldeng gi phur bu lnga gnad du btsugs te/dur khrod du mchis la dong brkos te/dong du bcug la steng du zhugs bus te/
16a4(rgyud): dug dang khrag dang /yungs kar bsres la lan brgya stong sbyin bsreg bgyis na/du zhing 'tshig ste 'phral du 'gum mo//mar gyi ngo zhag bcu bzhi lab ser mo'i lji ba sa la ma lhung 
16a5(rgyud): ba blangs te/sa phyogs gtsang mar gzugs bgyis la ci nus gyis mchod te zhag gcig bsnyung ba bgyis la/shing ba ling pa'i yaM shing bsreg cing zho dang /sbrang rtsi dang /
16a6(rgyud): mar la bsnyugs te/brgya stong sbyin bsreg bgyisan dpral(ba) las 'od zer 'byung ngo //de nas dngos grub du 'gyur te/gser dpag tu med pa rtsol bar 'gyur ro//yang na nyin gcig 
16a7(rgyud): bzhin du ti na ra rtsol bar 'gyur ro//gseg ma la lan brgya stong sngags ste/dur khrod du bor na//yi dgas thams cad glu len cing gar byed par 'gyur ro//mar gyi ngo zhag 
16a8(rgyud): bdun nas bcu bzhi nas/lag na rdo rje'i rkang pa'i mdun du zhag gsum bsnyung ba bgyis la/shingaka ra bi ra'i me tog la lan brgya stong sbyin bsreg bgyi'o//de nas ka ra bi ra'i 
16b1(rgyud): me tog dag las/me tog gcig blangs te/glu gnas sar song la bzlas brjod bgyis na/klu dang /klu mo rnams dbang du 'gyur te ci 'tshal ba rnyed do//lig bu mig dang /gser 
16b2(rgyud): dang /mchod phyir thogs kyang /ka ra bi ra'i me tog dang 'dra'o//gang na gnod sbyin mo 'dug pa de/bzlas brjod bgyis na/gnod sbyin mo rnams mchis te chung mar 'gyuro/
16b3(rgyud): yang na nor rtsol bar 'gyur ro//gal te mi mchi na zhag gcig bsnyung ba bgyis te/shing ma ta na'i tsher ma dang /dug dang khrag bsres la lan stong /seng ldeng gi zhugs 
16b4(rgyud): la sbyin bsreg bgyis na/de tshig cig mi sdug par 'gyur la 'chi'o//gal te klu mo mi mchi na/de la yang de nyid kyis sbyin bsreg bgyi'o//yar gyi ngo'i tshes bcwo lnga la zhag gsuM 
16b5(rgyud): bsnyung ba bgyis te/rgya mtsho 'bab pa'i chu khyor bar blugs la/klu shing gi me tog brgya stong sbyin bsreg bgyis na/dus ma yin par char 'babaso//zhag bdun 'o skyo 'thungs ste/
16b6(rgyud): mar gyi ngo zhag brgyad la zhag gcig bsnyung ba bgyi la/sa las bgyis pa'i rdo rje bzhag ste/de'i rkyed par shing a sho ta'i lo ma bdun bzhag la/de'i steng du ldong ros bzhag 
16b7(rgyud): ste/ci srid kyi sngon gsum du gyur pa de srid du bzlas brjod bgyi'o//'bar bar gyur na lo bdun stong du 'tsho'o//lus mdzes par bgyis la/mar gyi ngo zhag bcu bzhi 
16b8(rgyud): la nyin zhag gcig bsnyung ba bgyes te/lam gyi sum mdor mchod pa rgya chen po cher bgyis la/der 'dug ste ldong ros sran ma tsam gcig khar bcug la/ji srid gyi kha'i 
17a1(rgyud): rib mchog
17a2(rgyud): @#/ O /nang du gas par gyur pa de srid du bzlas brjod bgyi'o//de nas dngos grub du gyur te thig le bgyis na mi snang bar 'gyur ro//lo lnga brgya thub par 'gyur ro//
17a3(rgyud): ci 'dod pa'i gzugs can 'gyur ro//nyi ma'i gung tshigs la bgyi'o//tshes grangs dang skar ma 'dam mi mtshal/bsnyung ba bgyi mi 'tshal bar skyes pa'i ro ma smas ma nyams 
17a4(rgyud): pa blangs te/rdo rje ser mo'i gsang sngags bzlas brjod bgyis la bkru'o//rdo rje lte bas spos rtsol bar bgyi'o//de nas de'i snying khar 'dug ste/mdo tsam du gyen du ma 'phags gyi 
17a5(rgyud): bar du/rig pa mchog gi snying po dang lhan cig du bzlas brjod bgyi'o/gyen du 'phags ma thag tu slar 'og du ltung ngo //ltung na bud med bcu nye bar gnyas par gyur te/de dag 
17a6(rgyud): g.yog 'khor rnams la rtsal bar bgyi'o//de dag kyang stobs mnyam par 'gyuro//ci ste ma rtsal na/bdag bud med bcu yis bskor te/mi la zhon cing gar dga' ba der mchi'o//
17a7(rgyud): gang yidakyis bsam pa de bzhin pa las 'byung ngo //lo brgyad khrir 'tsho bar 'gyur ro//rig sngags 'chad du 'gyuro//mar gyi ngo zhag brgyad dam bcu bzhi la skyes pa shi ba de'i 
17a8(rgyud): ro bzhag pa'i phyogs kyis g.yas rol gyi zur shing khyer de song la/phyi ma mar gyi ngo zhag brgyad la/de mdung rtse gsum pa slongs mo pa'i zas bza' zhing /rig sngags bzlas 
17a9(rgyud): brjod bgyi'o//phyi ma mar gyi ngo zhag brgyadanas/zhag gsum bsnyung ba bgyis te/dur khrod du song la mdung rtse gsum pa las mchod pa rgya chen po bgyis te/mdung rtse gsum 
17b1(rgyud): pa lag pa g.yas su thogs la/rig pa mchog lan brgya stong bzlas brjod bgyis na/mdung rtse gsum pa las 'od zer 'byung ste/de nas dngos grub du 'gyur ro//mtshan mo btsugs 
17b2(rgyud): na rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa'i bud med 'byung ngo //skyes pa ci la ma reg pa chas pa dag kyang nye bar gnas so//gang bsam pa thams cad rnyed do//phyung na thams cad med 
17b3(rgyud): par 'gyur ro//'byung po rnams kyang phyag brnyan du 'gyur ro//cho ga gang la blangs pa'i cho ga de nyid la bsgrub par bgyi'o//mar gyi ngo zhag bcu la zhag gcig bsnyung bar bgyis te/

248b5(rgyud): mdun du lag ngar lhan cig du sbyar la/de nas sor mo phan tshun bsnol te/khu tshur bcwas nas gung mo bsgreng ste/gshibs la/de'i phyi rol du brla gnyis nas 'dzub mo gnyis rdo rje'i dbyibs su 
248b6(rgyud): byas/the bo gnyis rdo rje'i dbyibs ltar rab tu bzhag na ni/bcom ldan 'das rig pa mchog gi rtsa ba'i phyag rgya'o//de gnyis la 'the bo gnyis phral te bzhag pa ni rig sngags kyi rgyal po'i phyag rgya'o/

249a7(rgyud): ba ni/pad ma 'dzin pa'i phyag rgya'o//gsum len pa'i phyag rgya'o//de nyid lasayin no//cung zad brkyang pa ni dpal chen po'i phyag rgya'o//de nyid lasatshig dang po snying du gzhag pa ni/rdo rje mi g.yo

249b2(rgyud): nye ba'i snying po'i phyag rgya las de bzhin du 'dzub mo gnyis 'the bo las reg par byas pa ni/rdo rje khro bo mdzes pa'i phyag rgya'o//'dod pa'i rdo rje ma'i phyag rgya yang de nyid yinano//'dzub mo cung zad phye
